## 3. Tools and software used

The status column distinguishes tools used in the workflow from packages included in the environment or planned for use.

| Tool / software | Workflow area | Purpose | Where used | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Python | Core project scripting | Used to automate data processing, modelling workflows, docking/MD analysis and results documentation. | 04_code/scripts/ and related analysis scripts | Used |
| Conda / Miniforge | Environment management | Used to create and manage the tnik_md computational environment and its dependencies. | Project environment; environment configuration in 04_code/ | Used |
| RDKit | Chemical informatics | Used for molecular structure handling and Morgan fingerprint generation. | 03_processed_data/ and 05_models/ fingerprint workflow | Used |
| pandas | Tabular data processing | Used to clean, merge, filter and summarise ChEMBL activity data and model results. | 04_code/ scripts; 03_processed_data/; 09_results/ | Used |
| NumPy | Numerical computing | Used as a numerical computing dependency in the Python workflow. | 04_code/ scripts and modelling environment | Used as a supporting package |
| scikit-learn | Machine learning | Used for Random Forest regression, train/test splitting, cross-validation and model evaluation. | 05_models/rdkit_rf/ and 05_models/metrics/ | Used |
| Matplotlib | Scientific visualisation | Used to generate model, docking, MD and interaction-analysis figures. | 09_results/figures/ and analysis output folders | Used |
| Seaborn | Statistical visualisation | Available in the Python environment for statistical plotting. | 04_code/ environment | Environment package; individual use should be checked |
| SHAP | Model explainability | Included in the planned environment for interpreting model predictions. | 07_explainability/ | Environment/planned workflow; verify completed outputs |
| Transformers / PyTorch | Deep-learning support | Included in the planned environment for ChemBERTa or other deep-learning workflows. | 05_models/chemberta/ | Environment/planned workflow; do not assume model training was completed |
| ChEMBL | Bioactivity database | Source of TNIK activity records, compound identifiers, assay metadata and reported bioactivity evidence. | 02_raw_data/chembl/ and 03_processed_data/activity_clean/ | Used |
| AutoDock Vina | Molecular docking | Used for protein-ligand docking, virtual screening and repeated-seed docking calculations. | 06_docking/ | Used |
| Meeko | Docking preparation | Used in ligand/receptor preparation and the AutoDock Vina workflow. | 06_docking/receptor_prep/ and ligand preparation workflow | Used |
| Open Babel | Molecular conversion and preparation | Used for molecular file conversion and ligand protonation preparation. | 06_docking/molecular_dynamics/ligand preparation | Used |
| AmberTools / tleap | System preparation | Used to prepare protein-ligand systems, add solvent and ions, and generate Amber topology and coordinate files. | 06_docking/molecular_dynamics/ | Used |
| GAFF2 / parmchk2 | Ligand force-field parameterisation | Used to assign ligand force-field parameters and generate/check missing parameter files. | 06_docking/molecular_dynamics/ligand_parameterization/ | Used |
| GROMACS | Molecular dynamics | Used for energy minimisation, NVT and NPT simulations, trajectory processing and structural analysis. | 06_docking/molecular_dynamics/ | Used |
| PLIP | Protein-ligand interaction analysis | Used to identify and summarise interactions in representative docking poses. | 06_docking/interaction_analysis/ | Used |
| PyMOL | Molecular visualisation | Used for three-dimensional inspection and visualisation of protein-ligand structures and interaction poses. | 06_docking/interaction_analysis/comparative_analysis/3D_figures/ | Used |
| Biopython | Structural bioinformatics support | Included for handling biological sequence and structure data. | 04_code/ environment and structural workflow | Supporting package; individual script usage should be checked |
| JupyterLab | Interactive Python analysis | Included as an interactive notebook environment for project analysis. | 04_code/notebooks/ | Environment package; notebook usage should be checked |

## 4. Main project workflow

1. **ChEMBL data collection:** Collect TNIK bioactivity and assay records.
2. **Data cleaning:** Filter IC50 records, standardise units and calculate pIC50.
3. **Chemical fingerprints:** Generate 2048-bit Morgan fingerprints using RDKit.
4. **Machine learning:** Train and evaluate the Random Forest regression model.
5. **Model validation:** Perform held-out testing, cross-validation, scaffold validation and Y-randomisation.
6. **Structure preparation:** Prepare TNIK structure 6RA7 and ligand structures.
7. **Docking:** Validate docking by JWK redocking and screen compounds.
8. **Interaction analysis:** Analyse representative poses using PLIP and molecular visualisation.
9. **Ligand parameterisation:** Prepare protonation states and GAFF2 parameters.
10. **Molecular dynamics:** Run energy minimisation, restrained NVT and NPT equilibration.
11. **MD analysis:** Analyse RMSD, contacts, hydrogen bonds and residue occupancy.
12. **Results consolidation:** Combine ML, docking and MD evidence for cautious candidate prioritisation.

## 5. Important scientific notes

- Machine-learning performance is moderate and scaffold validation shows variability.
- Docking scores are computational predictions, not experimental binding affinities.
- The Ponatinib Kd evidence is from a Kinobeads cell-lysate competition assay; it does not prove purified-TNIK inhibition.
- The MD simulations are short restrained equilibration runs (500 ps NVT and 1 ns NPT), not unrestrained production simulations.
- Computational results do not establish therapeutic efficacy against IPF.
